Method: PCI建立在实际因果关系和Pearl的必要性/充分性概率概念之上，将可解释性问题重新表述为概率因果模型上的估计问题：通过指定"候选解释"的分布、反事实值的分布以及评分函数，利用蒙特卡洛方法进行高效近似，从而提供可计算、因果关系明确的分级解释。PCI将AC和Pearl的因果关系概率推广为退化（特例）情形。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界示例上进行了评估，包括与AC的一致性检验、扩展性实验、复杂的连续值动力系统，以及在数百万数据点上训练的真实部署因果机器学习模型。

Conclusion: PCI成功弥合了理论因果归因与可扩展归因方法之间的空白，提供了既保持因果严密性又可扩展到大规模真实世界场景的归因框架。

Abstract: Explaining why a specific outcome occurred, and which inputs deserve the blame or credit, is central to philosophical, scientific, and policy analysis. Existing tools split into two camps. The theory of actual causality (AC) gives principled verdicts, but only for toy-sized models, because computing them requires enumerating counterfactual scenarios. Scalable attribution methods like SHAP (or even causal SHAP) at least partially ignore the causal structure that generated the data, and can give answers that conflict with a careful causal analysis. We close this gap with Probabilistic Causal Impact (PCI).
  PCI builds on actual causality and on Pearl's notions of probability of necessity and sufficiency, but recasts the question of explainability as an estimation problem on a probabilistic causal model that is easily approximated via Monte Carlo. By specifying a distribution over "candidate explanations," a distribution over counterfactual values, and a scoring function, PCI provides tractable, causally grounded, graded explanations, generalizing AC and Pearl's probability of causation as degenerate cases.
  We evaluate PCI in synthetic and real-world examples, spanning consistency checks with AC, scaling experiments, complex continuous-valued dynamical systems, and a real-world deployed causal machine learning model trained on millions of datapoints.

</details>


### [69] [Clean Engineering, Unstable Measurement: A Preregistered Reliability Failure of Black-Box LLM Observers on Shared Endpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04198)
*Haoyaun Zhu,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对"语言模型裁判"作为测量工具的核心假设——同一请求发送到同一模型名称应产生一致读取——进行了审计。在预先注册的两轮测试中，52,988次请求显示同窗口重排序Spearman相关系数仅0.400（要求0.90），字节相同次日重放仅0.78（要求0.99），揭示共享服务基础设施上模型名称并非冻结的测量仪器。作者提出三机制解释、三级快照身份阶梯、八条设计规则及报告清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型裁判现被用于筛选训练数据、评判生成结果和驱动排行榜，其地位等同于测量仪器。然而其背后隐藏一个极少被检验的假设：相同的请求发送至相同模型名称，明天会读取到相同的结果。作者旨在审计这一基本前提的可靠性。

Method: 作者进行了两项预先注册的实验活动，所有阈值预先固定。共审计52,988次请求尝试，评估同窗口重复排名一致性（要求Spearman 0.90）与次日字节相同重放一致性（要求0.99）。随后进行预先注册的后续实验：等待时间是否有效、更换服务提供商（四家）是否有效、自托管批量不变内核、以及构造已知误差检验读取分离度。

Result: 两项门槛均未通过验证：同窗口重复排名Spearman为0.400（远低于要求的0.90），次日重放为0.78（低于要求的0.99），而执行记录均达上限。三种机制解释差距：标签到意义的映射偏差、候选差距低于仪器噪声底限七个数量级、字节相同输入产生不同排名。等待无帮助（0.805 vs 0.800）、更换提供商无帮助（四家中位数0.74-0.88）、自托管仅在服务器安静时有效、读取分离追踪误差类型而非大小。

Conclusion: 在共享端点上，模型名称不是冻结的仪器。预先注册的评估必须在冻结任何门槛之前先测量其仪器本身。研究提炼出三级快照身份阶梯、八条设计规则和报告清单，并指出约占总调用量2%的试点即可提前暴露两个不可达的门槛。

Abstract: Language-model judges now gate training data, score generations, and drive leaderboards. The judge is then a measurement instrument, resting on one rarely stated assumption: the same request, sent to the same model name, reads the same tomorrow. We audited that assumption in two preregistered campaigns with every threshold fixed in advance; neither got past validating its instrument. Across 52,988 audited request attempts, same-window repeat rankings agreed at Spearman 0.400 against a required 0.90, and byte-identical next-day replays agreed at 0.78 against a required 0.99, each time with the execution record at ceiling. Three mechanisms explain the gap: a label-to-meaning mapping that biased readouts as strongly as the signal; candidate gaps seven orders of magnitude below the instrument's own noise floor; and byte-identical inputs returning different rankings, a noise that exact-permutation readouts compound. Neither metric substitution nor sampling repaired it on the tested grid. Preregistered follow-ups bound the problem: waiting did not help on the days sampled (0.805 versus 0.800, replicated over five further days); switching providers did not help (four providers share the floor, medians 0.74 to 0.88, predicted by none of the metadata fields they expose); self-hosting on batch-invariant kernels helped only while the server was quiet; and on constructed errors with known gaps, the readout's separation tracks error type, not size. We distill the evidence into a three-level snapshot-identity ladder, eight design rules, and a reporting checklist; a pilot at roughly 2% of the study's call volume would have exposed both unreachable gates in advance. All results concern externally measured behaviour on shared serving infrastructure. On a shared endpoint, a model name is not a frozen instrument; a preregistered evaluation must measure its instrument before freezing any gate on it.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [70] [CHSR-RRF: A curriculum-gated hybrid retrieval framework with reciprocal rank fusion and leakage-aware benchmarking for educational RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02913)
*Terence Ateya,Zavier Ndum Ndum,Jicheng Fu,Kelly Tendongkeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CHSR-RRF课程门控混合检索框架和CERB基准数据集，解决教育问答中"课程泄漏"问题——即检索结果主题相关但不符合学科、年级或考试场景的约束。核心发现是：在检索前施加元数据约束相比检索后施加能显著减少泄漏（4.6倍），而检索后再加约束会导致召回率和精确范围成功率崩溃至零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RAG检索器仅优化主题相关性，忽视教育场景中的课程有效性约束。在学校环境中，一段内容即使主题相关，若来自错误的学科、水平或考试情境仍不适用，作者将这一失败模式定义为"课程泄漏"（curriculum leakage），并指出需要新的框架加以解决。

Method: 提出CHSR-RRF框架，在检索前施加元数据约束（课程门控），然后结合稀疏与稠密检索并使用倒数排名融合（reciprocal rank fusion）及确定性重排。同时引入CERB基准，包含126个注释案例，具有层级感知的相关性标签和显式泄漏标注。

Result: 在61例试点上，检索前门控使泄漏减少4.6倍（p<0.001）且保持排序召回率；而检索后施加同样约束导致召回率和精确范围成功率降至零（p=0.039）。全基准下限分析表明，许多剩余失败源于语料库和元数据的缺口，而非单纯的检索设计问题。

Conclusion: 在结构化教育领域中，检索应被视为约束性选择（constrained selection），有效性必须在候选池形成时执行，而非在排序之后补救。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is increasingly used in educational question answering, but standard retrievers optimize topical relevance without enforcing curriculum validity. In school settings, a passage can be relevant yet inappropriate if it comes from the wrong subject, level, or examination context; we call this failure mode curriculum leakage. We present CHSR-RRF, a curriculum-gated hybrid retrieval framework that applies metadata constraints before retrieval, then combines sparse and dense search with reciprocal rank fusion and deterministic reranking. We also introduce CERB, a 126-case benchmark for curriculum-constrained retrieval with hierarchy-aware relevance labels and explicit leakage annotations. On a 61-case pilot, pre-retrieval gating reduces leakage by 4.6x ($p<0.001$) while preserving ranked recall, whereas applying the same constraints after retrieval collapses recall and exact-scope success to zero ($p=0.039$). A full-benchmark lower-bound analysis further shows that many remaining failures arise from corpus and metadata gaps rather than retrieval design alone. These results show that retrieval in structured educational domains should be treated as constrained selection, with validity enforced when the candidate pool is formed rather than after ranking.

</details>


### [71] [Reflect-SQL: A Self-Reflection Based Framework for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02944)
*Anupreksha Jain,Manish Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Reflect-SQL框架，通过多阶段自我反思机制解决Text-to-SQL在实际企业应用中的三大挑战（模式晦涩、检索低效、SQL生成缺陷），在BIRD基准上达到72.03%的执行准确率，显著超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代企业希望通过自然语言实现数据访问的民主化，但Text-to-SQL的实际采用受到现实场景中三大系统性挑战的阻碍：数据库模式晦涩且庞大、结构化模式与模糊用户查询导致表和列的检索效率低下、缺乏稳健验证机制导致生成语法或逻辑错误的SQL。现有单次生成的方法无法应对这些复杂情况。

Method: Reflect-SQL采用多阶段自我反思框架，包含三个关键组件：1) 利用知识库构建对晦涩模式的理解；2) 建立有效的检索流程；3) 生成语法和语义正确的SQL。系统通过LLM-as-a-judge驱动的评分机制在互联反馈回路中迭代优化每个阶段。具体包括：反馈驱动的检索循环来精炼用户自然语言查询、合成循环来验证和纠正SQL、蕴含循环来优化端到端流程并持续丰富知识库。

Result: 在具有挑战性的BIRD基准上，Reflect-SQL实现了72.03%的执行准确率，显著优于最先进的基线方法，展示了企业应用可靠性的重大提升。

Conclusion: 通过整合多层反思机制，Reflect-SQL弥合了用户意图与复杂数据之间的关键鸿沟，为Text-to-SQL在企业场景中的实用化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Democratizing data access through natural language is a crucial goal for modern enterprises, but the practical adoption of Text-to-SQL is critically hindered by real-world complexities: 1. Obscure and large database schemas, 2. Ineffective retrieval of relevant tables and columns due to structured setting of schemas and vague user query, 3. Generation of syntactically or logically flawed SQL due to a lack of robust validation and correction mechanism. To address these systemic challenges, we introduce Reflect-SQL, a novel framework for Text to SQL, grounded in multi-stage self-reflection approach to develop understanding of obscure schema using a knowledge base, setup a process for effective retrieval and system to generate syntactically/semantically SQL. Instead of a single-pass attempt, our system employs an LLM-as-a-judge driven scoring mechanism within interconnected feedback loops to iteratively refine the results at every stage. A feedback-driven retrieval loop refines the user's natural language query, while a synthesis loop validates and corrects the SQL and finally, an entailment loop optimizes the end-to-end process and continuously enriches the knowledge base. By integrating these layers of reflection, Reflect-SQL bridges the critical gap between user intent and complex data. On the challenging BIRD benchmark, our framework achieves an execution accuracy of 72.03%, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baselines, demonstrating a major leap in reliability for enterprise applications.

</details>


### [72] [UniCon: A Unified Context-Centric Modeling Paradigm for CTR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03290)
*Jiajun Cui,Zhengqi Xu,Fan Zhang,Zhangteng,Gu Tang,Honghong Zhu,Mengxi Wu,Yulin Liang,Xingxing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出UniCon，一种统一的上下文中心（context-centric）建模架构，将请求上下文作为基本建模单元，把历史行为和预测目标组织为同构的上下文单元，通过上下文内注意力（Locality）和上下文间注意力（Dynamics）分别捕捉上下文内的物品局部耦合与跨上下文的决策状态动态演化，从而弥合历史与目标之间的结构鸿沟。在美团搜索广告中，UniCon相较生产基线离线AUC提升0.0139，线上RPM提升3.09%、CTR提升2.07%、收入提升2.95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业级CTR统一建模方法通常在token级别统一序列信号和非序列信号，通过共享骨干网络建模交互并增大模型容量来提升扩展能力。然而这种划分源自传统的特征工程实践，与底层决策过程不一致：用户行为本质上是同质上下文单元构成的序列，历史和当前请求的差异仅在于其结果是否已观测。将其视为异构信号会掩盖用户决策上下文中的结构依赖，制约扩展效率和预测质量，尤其在电商货架和瀑布流等上下文丰富的场景中更为明显。

Method: UniCon将请求上下文（request context）作为基本建模单元，将历史行为和预测目标统一组织为同质的上下文单元。具体包含两种注意力机制：一是上下文内注意力（Intra-context attention），用于捕捉同一上下文内物品间的局部耦合（Locality）；二是上下文间注意力（Inter-context attention），用于建模不同上下文间决策状态的动态演化（Dynamics）。此外还引入上下文单元级别的序列压缩以降低部署开销。

Result: 在美团搜索广告场景中，UniCon相较于强生产基线在离线AUC上提升0.0139；线上实验取得统计显著的提升：RPM提升3.09%，CTR提升2.07%，收入提升2.95%。

Conclusion: UniCon通过将建模视角从token级异质信号统一转向上下文级同质单元，弥合了历史行为与预测目标之间的结构性鸿沟，在上下文丰富的实际工业场景中同时提升了预测质量、扩展效率和部署效率，验证了上下文中心建模范式在统一CTR预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Unified modeling has become a major direction for industrial click-through rate (CTR) prediction. Existing approaches typically unify sequential and non-sequential signals at the token level, model their interactions in a shared backbone, and increase model capacity to improve scaling behavior. However, this division originates from legacy feature-engineering practice and is misaligned with the underlying decision process. User behavior is inherently a sequence of homogeneous context units; at the level of input organization, historical behavior and the current request differ only in whether their outcomes are observed or remain to be predicted. Treating them as heterogeneous signals obscures structural dependencies within the user's decision context, limiting both scaling efficiency and prediction quality. This limitation is particularly pronounced in context-rich scenarios such as e-commerce shelves and waterfall feeds. To address this, we propose UniCon, a unified context-centric modeling architecture that treats the request context as the basic modeling unit and organizes history and prediction targets as homogeneous context units. Intra-context attention captures local coupling among items within a context (Locality), while inter-context attention models the dynamic evolution of decision states across contexts (Dynamics). This organization bridges the structural gap between history and target and supports more effective scaling of unified CTR models. Context-unit-level sequence compression further reduces deployment overhead. On Meituan search advertising, UniCon improves offline AUC by 0.0139 over a strong production baseline and achieves statistically significant online lifts of 3.09% in RPM, 2.07% in CTR, and 2.95% in revenue.

</details>


### [73] [DoPR: Reusable Compressed Document Prefixes for Efficient LLM Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03311)
*Beiya Dai,Yifan Wei,Guang Yang,Xing Shi,Xinbing Wang,Zhouhan Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DoPR框架，通过离线预计算压缩文档前缀状态，将文档侧计算从在线重排序中解耦，实现最多8.04倍延迟加速和8倍文档侧显存缩减，同时保持97.1%-99.5%的NDCG@10性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型（LLM）是有效的重排序器，但逐点（pointwise）重排序方式在处理不同查询时需要反复处理同一文档，导致大量冗余的文档侧计算，计算效率和资源利用率低下。

Method: DoPR框架包含两个阶段：(1) 离线阶段，选取查询无关的文档表示并转换为压缩文档前缀状态并预计算存储；(2) 在线阶段，重排序时仅处理查询和评分token，通过存储的前缀状态提供文档信息，实现跨查询的前缀状态复用和文档侧压缩。

Result: 在TREC DL、BEIR和BRIGHT数据集上，使用Qwen3模型（0.6B至8B）进行实验，DoPR实现了最多8.0倍的在线文档侧显存缩减和8.04倍的延迟加速，同时保留了对照组完整文档重排序器平均NDCG@10的97.1%-99.5%。

Conclusion: DoPR通过离线在线解耦的压缩文档前缀框架，有效消除了跨查询的冗余文档侧计算，在保持重排序质量的前提下显著降低了在线计算开销，验证了文档前缀压缩与状态复用策略的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are effective rerankers, but pointwise reranking repeatedly processes the same document across different queries, causing substantial redundant document-side computation. We propose \textbf{DoPR}, a compressed document prefix framework that decouples offline document processing from online reranking. DoPR first selects query-independent document representations and converts them into compressed document prefix states, which are precomputed offline and reused whenever the document is retrieved. During online reranking, the model scores each query-document pair by processing only the query and scoring token, with document information supplied by the stored prefix states. This design reduces online cost through both document-side compression and cross-query prefix-state reuse. Experiments on TREC DL, BEIR, and BRIGHT with Qwen3 models from $0.6$B to $8$B show that DoPR achieves up to 8.0$\times$ online document-side memory reduction and up to 8.04$\times$ latency speedup, while retaining \textbf{97.1\%-99.5\%} of the average NDCG@10 of matched full-document rerankers.

</details>


### [74] [SelfDR: Self-Distillation from Reasoning for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03313)
*Chumeng Jiang,Jiayin Wang,Xinjie Lin,Zhiqiang Guo,Hengliang Luo,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SelfDR是一个自蒸馏框架，通过将LLM自身带有推理过程的预测蒸馏为直接推荐预测，在保持推理效率的同时提升推荐准确性，所有组件均基于同一个基础LLM，无需外部模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在推荐系统中表现出色，但显式生成中间推理轨迹的计算成本过高，限制了其在实际推荐系统中的部署。

Method: SelfDR框架包含两个核心组件：1）教师推荐器——通过训练reasoner（以下游性能为奖励）生成针对性推理，并将推理结果融入教师输入；2）学生推荐器——基于同一底层LLM学习直接推荐，通过动态权重策略从教师处进行自蒸馏。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的大量实验验证了SelfDR在有效性、合理性和效率方面的优越表现。

Conclusion: SelfDR成功实现了LLM推荐系统中推理增强与推理效率之间的平衡，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently emerged as powerful backbones for recommendation. To better elicit their capabilities, reasoning has been widely incorporated to help LLMs interpret rich textual signals and improve recommendation accuracy. However, explicitly generating intermediate reasoning traces often incurs substantial computational costs, which limits practical deployment in real-world recommender systems. To address this challenge, we propose SelfDR, a Self-Distillation from Reasoning framework for LLM-based Recommendation. SelfDR distills an LLM's own reasoning-enhanced predictions to produce recommendations directly, improving recommendation effectiveness while maintaining inference efficiency. All components in the framework are built on the same base LLM, without relying on any external models. Specifically, the teacher recommender is constructed by training a reasoner with downstream performance as the reward, enabling it to generate targeted rationales that are later incorporated into the teacher's input. A student recommender for direct recommendation, with the same underlying model, then learns from the teacher through self-distillation with a dynamic weighting strategy. Extensive experiments on three public datasets validate the effectiveness, rationality, and efficiency of SelfDR. Codes are available at https://github.com/JiangDeccc/SelfDistillation.

</details>


### [75] [SciLENS: RL-Driven Autonomous Agents for Scientific Localized Evidence Navigation and Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03338)
*Leqi Zheng,Jinbo Su,Yuying Li,Chaokun Wang,Weiping Wang,Haitao Li,Jiajun Zhang,Shannan Yan,Zhaolu Kang,Rong Fu,Jie Wu,Fang Niu,Hang Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SciLENS是一个完全本地的自主科学文献综述代理框架，基于约1200万条学术记录的双层基础设施构建，通过将结构化可视化集成到推理循环中压缩复杂的引文拓扑，并借助自动数据合成管道和反向分解奖励策略进行无人工标注训练，在六个科学基准上显著优于开源基线，性能可比肩GPT-5.2和Gemini-3.0-pro。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学文献综述代理依赖专有在线服务，存在可复现性差、隐私泄露风险和无法离线部署等局限，亟需一个完全本地的替代方案。

Method: 1) 提出双层基础设施，索引约1200万学术记录；2) 将结构可视化作为推理循环中的可操作工具，将复杂引文拓扑压缩为经数据验证的图表，缓解宏观综述时的上下文耗尽问题；3) 构建无需人工标注的自动数据合成管道，从引文知识图中提取多跳子图，并经20个前沿模型的跨模型共识验证；4) 通过反向分解评估策略（reverse-decomposition rubric）进行对齐，提供细粒度过程奖励以实现早期规划和严格的证据基础。

Result: 在涵盖标准问答、引文准确性、事实推理和结构综合的六个科学基准上的评估显示，SciLENS显著优于开源基线，并达到与GPT-5.2和Gemini-3.0-pro相当的性能。

Conclusion: SciLENS验证了完全本地化的自主科学综述代理的可行性，证明了结构可视化增强推理、无标注训练策略及细粒度过程奖励对齐等方法的有效性，其代码和数据集已开源以促进可复现研究。

Abstract: Scientific literature synthesis agents increasingly rely on proprietary online services, limiting reproducibility, privacy, and offline deployment. To address this challenge, we introduce SciLENS Scientific Localized Evidence Navigation and Synthesis), a fully local autonomous agent framework operating on a dual-tier infrastructure indexing approximately 12 million academic records. SciLENS pioneers the integration of structural visualization as an actionable tool within the reasoning loop, enabling the agent to compress complex citation topologies into validated data-driven charts and thereby mitigate context exhaustion during macro-level synthesis. To train the agent without human annotation, we develop an automated data synthesis pipeline that extracts multi-hop subgraphs from a citation knowledge graph, verified by cross-model consensus among 20 frontier models. The agent is subsequently aligned through a reverse-decomposition rubric strategy that provides fine-grained process rewards for early planning and strict evidence grounding. Evaluations across six scientific benchmarks encompassing standard QA, citation accuracy, factual reasoning, and structural synthesis demonstrate that SciLENS significantly outperforms open-source baselines and achieves performance comparable to GPT-5.2 and Gemini-3.0-pro. Our source code and data are released at https://github.com/LQgdwind/SciLENS.

</details>


### [76] [HypRQ-VAE: Hyperbolic Item Indexing for Long-Tail-Aware Generative Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03369)
*Longfeng Wu,Tong Zeng,Giovanni Seni,Zhimin Peng,Bhanu Pratap Singh Rawat,Si Zhang,Yao Zhou,Lecheng Zheng,Bo Ji,Yujun Yan,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出HypRQ-VAE，首个在双曲空间中学习物品索引的框架，用于生成式推荐系统。通过利用双曲几何的指数体积扩张特性来适配物品的长尾分布，缓解LLM生成推荐中文本token与离散物品索引之间的失配导致的幻觉问题。在三个基准数据集上显著提升推荐性能，尤其在尾部物品推荐上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐系统使用LLM将推荐视为语言建模任务，但存在根本性失配：LLM基于文本token操作，而推荐系统依赖离散物品索引，导致幻觉现象。现有方法在欧氏空间学习物品词汇表，但难以建模真实目录中固有的长尾分布——少量头部物品主导，大量尾部物品反映用户细分偏好。

Method: 提出Hyperbolic Residual-Quantized Variational AutoEncoder (HypRQ-VAE)，首次在双曲空间中学习物品索引。利用双曲几何的指数体积扩张特性天然适配用户-物品交互的幂律结构，既能编码丰富的文本语义，又能保持稀疏尾部物品的表征保真度。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，HypRQ-VAE显著提升推荐性能，尤其在推荐尾部物品方面改善明显。分析显示这些提升源于双曲空间在生成式推荐中建模物品层次结构和稀疏性的卓越能力。

Conclusion: 双曲空间为生成式推荐中物品索引学习提供了一种优于欧氏空间的解决方案，能够有效处理长尾分布并提升尾部物品推荐效果。代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Sequential recommender systems model user behavior as item ID sequences, while recent generative methods cast recommendation as a language modeling task using large language models (LLMs). While this paradigm incorporates rich textual semantics, it introduces a fundamental mismatch: LLMs operate on text tokens, whereas recommender systems depend on discrete item indices. This misalignment often leads to hallucinations in generative recommendations. Existing methods attempt to bridge this gap by learning item vocabularies in Euclidean space, but they struggle to model the inherent long-tail distribution of real-world catalogs, where a small number of head items dominate, and a vast number of tail items reflect users' niche preferences. To address this issue, we introduce Hyperbolic Residual-Quantized Variational AutoEncoder (HypRQ-VAE), the first framework to learn item indexing in hyperbolic space. HypRQ-VAE leverages the unique properties of hyperbolic geometry, whose exponential volume expansion naturally accommodates the power law structure of user-item interactions. This allows the model to encode rich textual semantics while preserving the representational fidelity of sparse, long-tail items. Experiments on three benchmark datasets show that HypRQ-VAE significantly improves the performance of recommendation, particularly in recommending tail items. Our analysis attributes these gains to the superior capacity of hyperbolic space to model item hierarchies and sparsity in generative recommendation. Our code and data are available at: https://github.com/wulongfeng/HypRQ-VAE.

</details>


### [77] [Plan Pointers and Record-Directive Form in Budgeted Verification of Inherited Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03450)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究考察智能体在继承六条单行记忆后，通过最多拉取一条归档源记录来决定行动的场景。研究发现，存储在库中的指令（包括ID指针、识别标准或二者结合）可以引导智能体的记录选择。多个受控研究显示不同模型对标准与ID的反应存在显著差异，包括在Claude系列模型上附加ID会取消标准效果的现象，总体结果仅为描述性效应而非机制性声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在受限记忆约束下，外部存储指令（指针/标准）如何影响智能体的档案检索选择，以及不同模型族对此类指令的遵循差异，为模型行为的一致性与可控性提供经验证据。

Method: 通过12项注册研究（14,760次尝试），使用同一工具谱系，系统比较三种指令形式（唯一ID指针、匹配标准、二者结合）对智能体记录拉取行为的影响。研究在6个直接提供方模型和9个OpenRouter模型面板上进行，运用长度匹配对照、逐字节精确编辑、前瞻性注册重跑等设计，测量指令形式对目标记录选择率的效应。

Result: 直接提供方模型上，长度匹配的标准比裸ID高+35.0点[+31.2,+38.8]；但在OpenRouter面板上未通过预先注册的优越性规则。在Claude模型上，ID附加到标准会取消标准效果（如Opus 5从40/40降至0/40）；六种字节匹配编辑对各精确字符串产生独立效应；重跑后15/30个复制对比在边际范围内，15个未定，无一超出。批准行（+96.0点）与双点预算可恢复目标；标准后缀的取消效应在Opus 5上适用于4/5措辞，在Fable 5.1上适用于全部5种。第二存储中所有模型都遵循标准。决策场景中，标准在Opus 5上使选择移向当前记录（+100.0点），在Fable 5.1上则远离。单字符计划指针效应（+78.0点）在前瞻注册重跑中得到确认（+81.7点）。

Conclusion: 指令形式（ID vs. 标准 vs. 结合）对智能体的档案检索行为有显著且模型依赖的影响；标准在多数直接提供方模型上优于ID，但该优势未在更广泛的模型面板上复现；某些模型对"标准+ID"组合会产生意外的取消效应。所有结果均为固定面板上精确编辑的注册区间描述性效应，不涉及机制层面的因果主张。

Abstract: An agent that inherits six one-line memories may pull at most one archived source record before acting; a directive written into the store can steer that choice: a pointer to the record, a criterion that identifies it, or both. Across twelve registered studies on one instrument lineage (14,760 attempts) we measured where the request goes under each form. On six direct-provider models a length-matched criterion exceeded a bare id by +35.0 points [+31.2, +38.8] (Study D); the contrast failed its registered superiority rule on a nine-model OpenRouter-served panel (Study E). Appending the id cancelled the criterion on three Claude models (Opus 5: 40/40 to 0/40; Study F-x); six byte-matched edits gave each exact string its own effect (Study G), and a re-run at eighty runs per cell left fifteen of thirty replication contrasts within the margin, fifteen unresolved and none beyond (Study G'). A ratification line (+96.0 points on Opus 5) and a budget of two credits restored the target on all three (Study J); across five criterion strings the suffix's cancellation held for four of the five wordings on Opus 5 and all five wordings on Fable 5.1 (Study H2); in a second store every model followed the criterion (Study H1). Continued into a decision, the criterion moved the choice toward the current record (+100.0 points, Opus 5) and away from it on Fable 5.1 (Study I). A one-character plan pointer's effect (+78.0 points; Study B, after a correction of its first repository report) returned the same verdict under a prospectively registered re-run (+81.7 points; Study B'). All results are descriptive effects of exact edits on fixed panels with registered intervals and no mechanism claim.

</details>


### [78] [ExplainRoute: A Pre-Deployment Audit Framework for Non-Answer-Giving Programming Tutors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03470)
*Yiming Gai,Yingying Zhang,Xuefei Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ExplainRoute，一个用于非答案型编程辅导系统的部署前审计框架。它在代码行和学习者解释的基础上估计解释状态，并在两类有界响应（Feynman式自我解释提示或Socratic脚手架）中选择。该框架通过机器可验证的契约暴露状态、策略、引用代码片段和泄漏风险，并在SelfCode语料库的1,770对数据上离线评估，验证了审计协议的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程辅导系统应支持学习者自身的解释而非直接给出模型答案，但现有的基准仅按流畅度对辅导系统排名，缺乏能够审计信息边界、响应极性、失败闭包以及学习者解释可见性价值的体系化评估方法。作者希望在课堂部署之前，提供一种可验证的审计框架来确保系统的教学安全性。

Method: ExplainRoute采用两阶段自适应路由机制：给定代码行和学习者解释，首先估计解释状态（Open或Socratic），然后选择对应的有界响应类型。框架通过机器可检查的契约暴露其内部状态、策略选择、引用的代码片段以及信息泄漏风险。评估在SelfCode语料库的1,770对数据上进行，采用代码组划分并预留11个未接触的保持组共443对。对比了五种条件：直接回答、固定开放性自我解释、固定Socratic脚手架、自适应路由及无状态消融。

Result: 所有教学条件下的契约有效性达到100%。自适应路由在60.5%的记录上与冻结参考规则一致，状态宏F1为0.238（Open:0.229; Socratic:0.246），未显示可靠的自适应优势。独立语言模型裁判对自适应响应评分为4.516/5，优于无状态消融（2.819/5），但略低于固定开放性自我解释（4.598/5）和Socratic脚手架（4.658/5）。盲法评分评估在分层40行子集上证实，可见的学习者解释提升了信息价值，但自适应路由未优于固定策略。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于提供了一个经过验证的审计协议和一个边界发现，而非学习提升、记忆保持或因果教学有效性的证据。核心结论是：可见的学习者解释能改善信息价值，但当前的自适应路由并不优于固定的教学策略。

Abstract: Programming tutors should support learners' own explanations rather than immediately providing model answers. We present ExplainRoute, a pre-deployment audit framework for non-answer-giving programming tutors. Given a code line and a learner explanation, it estimates the explanation state and selects one of two bounded responses: a Feynman-style self-explanation prompt or a Socratic scaffold. The framework exposes its state, strategy, cited code fragment, and leakage risk through a machine-checkable contract. Unlike benchmarks that rank tutors by fluency alone, ExplainRoute audits information boundaries, response polarity, failure closure, and the value of learner-explanation visibility before classroom deployment. We evaluate it offline on the 1,770-pair SelfCode corpus using a code-group split, with 443 pairs reserved in 11 untouched holdout groups. The evaluation compares direct answers, fixed open self-explanation, fixed Socratic scaffolding, adaptive routing, and an adaptive no-state ablation. Contract validity reaches 100% for all pedagogical conditions. Adaptive routing matches the frozen reference rule on 60.5% of records, with state macro-F1 of 0.238 (Open: 0.229; Socratic: 0.246), showing no reliable adaptive advantage. An independent language-model judge scores adaptive responses 4.516/5, outperforming the no-state ablation (2.819/5) but slightly below fixed open self-explanation (4.598/5) and Socratic scaffolding (4.658/5). A blinded rubric evaluation on a stratified 40-row subset confirms that visible learner explanations improve information value while adaptive routing does not outperform fixed strategies. The contribution is a validated audit protocol and a boundary finding, rather than evidence of improved learning, retention, or causal instructional effectiveness.

</details>


### [79] [From Topical Relevance to Answerability: Entailment Distillation for Conversational Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03482)
*Shuai Qin,Guojia An,Weikang Guo,Pei Ke,Jiwei Wei,Yang Yang,Jie Zou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CLEAR框架，将对话式检索从主题相关性转向可回答性，通过蕴涵蒸馏和反事实回忆模块，在TopiOCQA、QReCC和TREC CAsT数据集上显著提升检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式检索器将主题相关性视为可回答性的代理指标，但主题匹配的段落不一定是支撑正确答案的段落，存在系统的可回答性差距（answerability gap）。

Method: CLEAR框架包含两个核心模块：(1) 蕴涵蒸馏（entailment distillation），将答案-段落蕴涵监督信号迁移到交叉编码器重排序器，使推理时不依赖答案即可区分支撑答案的段落和主题干扰项；(2) 以段落为中心的反事实回忆模块（passage-centric abductive recall），利用LLM从段落反向推断可回答的查询，将低相似度但可回答的段落纳入候选池。

Result: 在TopiOCQA、QReCC及域外TREC CAsT数据集上，CLEAR一致性地优于强查询重写和稠密检索基线，在主题噪声更重的对话中提升最大；将重排序器应用于LLM驱动的查询重写器之上可进一步获益。

Conclusion: 从主题相关性到可回答性的范式转变能有效弥合对话检索中的答案性差距，尤其在富含主题干扰的复杂对话场景下表现突出。

Abstract: Existing conversational retrievers commonly treat topical relevance as a proxy for answerability. However, a passage that closely matches the dialogue context is not necessarily the one that supports the correct answer. We identify this mismatch as a systematic answerability gap. To address this issue, we propose CLEAR, a framework that shifts conversational retrieval from topical relevance to answerability. The core of CLEAR is entailment distillation, which transfers answer-passage entailment supervision into a cross-encoder reranker so that the reranker discriminates answer-supporting passages from topical distractors at inference time, without requiring answers. CLEAR is complemented by a passage-centric abductive recall module that brings low-similarity yet answerable passages into the candidate pool by inferring answerable queries from passages with an LLM. Across TopiOCQA, QReCC, and out-of-domain TREC CAsT datasets, CLEAR consistently improves top-ranked precision over strong query-rewriting and dense-retrieval baselines, with the largest gains observed in conversations involving heavier topical noise. Moreover, applying our reranker on top of an LLM-driven query rewriter yields further gains.

</details>


### [80] [EPIC: Explicit Posterior Item Conditioning for Semantic ID Diffusion Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03522)
*Tuan-Binh Tran,Thanh Tam Nguyen,Quoc Viet Hung Nguyen,Dung D. Le,Tung Kieu,Thanh Trung Huynh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出EPIC方法，在语义ID生成的掩码扩散推荐过程中引入显式物品级竞争，通过基于当前生成上下文和用户近期交互构建个性化后验分布，并将该分布投影回未解析的SID位置来引导token决策。预训练骨干网络保持冻结，无需额外解码器前向传播，在四个Amazon基准上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有掩码扩散SID推荐方法虽然在双向上下文和灵活解码上有优势，但在每一步去噪时，部分SID可能对应多个可行物品，而现有方法主要依赖逐位置token预测进行推理，缺乏对完整物品候选项的显式考量，导致推理过程中可能丢失有前景的物品假设。

Method: EPIC在SID去噪过程中引入显式物品级竞争：首先利用当前生成上下文和用户近期交互构建可行候选物品的个性化后验分布，然后将该分布投影回未解析的SID位置，以此引导后续token决策。该方法保持预训练骨干网络冻结，且不需要额外的解码器前向传播。

Result: 在四个Amazon基准数据集上的实验显示，EPIC相较强基线取得了一致的性能提升。诊断分析表明，增益主要来源于个性化转移证据，该证据在去噪过程中能够保留有前景的物品假设。

Conclusion: 将物品级后验信息显式注入SID去噪过程是提升生成式推荐性能的有效途径，且无需修改预训练模型即可实现改进，为掩码扩散推荐方法提供了新的改进方向。

Abstract: Semantic ID (SID) generative recommendation predicts the next item by generating a short tuple of discrete tokens. Recent masked-diffusion methods improve this process through bidirectional context and flexible decoding, yet recommendation ultimately requires selecting among complete catalog items. At each denoising step, a partial SID can correspond to multiple feasible items, while existing methods primarily reason through position-wise token predictions. We propose Explicit Posterior Item Conditioning (EPIC), which introduces explicit item-level competition into SID denoising. EPIC constructs a personalized posterior over feasible candidate items using the current generation context and the user's recent interactions, then projects this distribution back to unresolved SID positions to guide subsequent token decisions. The pretrained backbone remains frozen and requires no additional decoder forward pass. Experiments on four Amazon benchmarks show consistent improvements over strong baselines, while diagnostic analyses indicate that the gains primarily arise from personalized transition evidence that preserves promising item hypotheses during denoising.

</details>


### [81] [LLM4AIGQ: LLM-based AI Guidance Query Generation Framework for Multi Interest Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03674)
*Xiangchen Pan,Jiayi Xu,Jing Wang,Xing Fang,Lingyun Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM4AIGQ，一种基于大型语言模型（LLM）的电商AI引导查询生成方案。该方法通过整合用户画像和历史交互序列进行多兴趣兴趣细分，为每个子兴趣推断具体消费意图并生成对应的AI引导查询（AIGQ），以解决传统Q2AIGQ两阶段范式中的语义漂移和用户多兴趣探索不足问题。训练采用SFT、RL和DPO的后训练流水线，并设计了多层级奖励机制以支持多目标优化和长链推理；部署上采用近线生成、在线读取的架构以满足延迟约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统"查询到AI生成查询"（Q2AIGQ）两阶段关联范式存在两大缺陷：一是多路径检索到规则泛化过程中发生信息级联损失导致语义漂移；二是主搜索查询推导高度依赖"用户-物品"共现关系，缺乏对用户多兴趣的探索，导致引导查询价值低且与购买意图不匹配。因此需要一种能突破共现关系表达局限、覆盖用户多兴趣的引导查询生成方法。

Method: 1) 利用LLM整合用户画像与历史交互序列进行用户兴趣细分；2) 为每个子兴趣推断具体消费意图并生成对应AIGQ；3) 训练阶段采用包含SFT、RL和DPO的后训练流水线；4) 设计多层级奖励机制以满足多目标优化与长链推理需求；5) 部署采用近线生成与在线读取架构以控制延迟。

Result: 离线评估与在线A/B测试均表明模型在AIGQ生成任务上取得了稳健的性能表现，验证了LLM4AIGQ在引导查询质量和购买意图匹配方面的有效性。

Conclusion: LLM4AIGQ通过基于LLM的多兴趣引导查询生成方案有效克服了传统共现关系检索方法的语义局限，在模型训练采用多阶段后训练流水线并结合多层级奖励设计，实现了引导查询生成的性能提升，同时在工程部署上兼顾了延迟约束。

Abstract: Guidance queries stimulate user consumption by extracting preferences to provide search queries with guidance value, playing a crucial role in the e-commerce field. Traditional AI-generated queries (AIGQ) generation primarily relies on a two-stage "Query-to-AI-Generated-Query" (Q2AIGQ) association paradigm, first recalling user primary search queries from user profiles, historical behavior sequences, item-side information, and the current query through multi-path retrieval, then generalizing AIGQ via rule-based methods. This approach suffers from semantic drift due to information cascade loss; additionally, primary search query derivation heavily depends on "user-item" co-occurrence relationships, lacking exploration of user multi-interests, resulting in guidance queries with low value and mismatched purchase intent. To address the expressive limitations of traditional co-occurrence-based retrieval, we propose LLM4AIGQ, an LLM-based solution for generating AI guidance queries tailored to users' multi-interests. This approach segments user interests by integrating user profiles and historical interaction sequences, infers specific consumption intents for each sub-interest, and subsequently generates corresponding AIGQ. In terms of model training, we employ a post-training pipeline comprising Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), Reinforcement Learning (RL), and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to enhance the model's capability in generating AIGQ. We also introduce a multi-level reward design to satisfy the requirements of multi-objective optimization and long-chain reasoning in practical applications. Regarding deployment, we adopt a nearline-generation and online-read architecture to meet latency constraints. Extensive experimental analyses demonstrate that our model achieves robust performance in both offline evaluations and online A/B tests.

</details>


### [82] [Unified Pitch Graphs for Diagnosing Pitching Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03810)
*Kichang Lee,JeongGil Ko*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出统一投球图（UPG），一种用于棒球投球策略回顾性分析的分层图表示，将每次投球保留为具有三维轨迹和上下文的确切事件，通过有向序列边连接连续投球，并在语义和时间分辨率上组织相同事件。支持自适应机制在重复证据不足时从细粒度长序列回退，同时保留事件谱系。在394万条MLB Statcast数据上，支持自适应回退将保留路径覆盖率从18.9%提升至94.9%，执行重建R²从0.495提升至0.685。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有棒球投球策略表示方法将投球压缩为离散类型或聚合统计，丢失了物理执行细节与投球顺序上下文之间的丰富关联，无法在细粒度水平上刻画投球策略的完整结构。

Method: 提出统一投球图（UPG），一种分层图表示，将每个投球保留为带重建三维轨迹和上下文的精确事件，通过有向序列边连接连续投球，并在不同语义和时间分辨率上组织相同事件。设计支持自适应（support-adaptive）回退机制，当重复证据不足时从精细长序列退回到更粗的表示，同时保留精确的事件谱系。

Result: 在2021至2026年394万条MLB Statcast投球数据上评估：名义上相同的投球序列表现出不同的物理执行；有序结构在更长上下文条件下愈发明显。支持自适应回退将保留路径覆盖率从18.9%提高到94.9%，执行重建R²从0.495提升到0.685。UPG还能可靠定位受控执行变化，这是离散投球组合和序列表示无法检测的。

Conclusion: UPG提供了可溯源、多尺度的投球策略表示，能够识别重复出现的策略模式，同时避免将回顾性关联与因果或未来表现声明相混淆。

Abstract: Pitching strategy in baseball is expressed through both physical execution and the ordered context in which pitches are used, yet common representations collapse pitches into discrete types or aggregate statistics. We present Unified Pitch Graphs (UPG), a hierarchical graph representation for retrospective analysis of sequential spatiotemporal events. UPG preserves each pitch as an exact event with reconstructed three-dimensional trajectory and context, connects consecutive pitches through directed sequence edges, and organizes the same events across semantic and temporal resolutions. A support-adaptive mechanism backs off from fine, long sequences when repeated evidence is insufficient, while retaining exact event lineage. We evaluate UPG on 3.94 million MLB Statcast pitches from 2021 to 2026. Nominally identical pitch sequences exhibit distinct physical executions, and ordered structure becomes increasingly evident in longer context-conditioned paths. Support-adaptive backoff increases held-out path coverage from 18.9\% to 94.9\% while improving execution reconstruction from $R^2=0.495$ to $0.685$. UPG also reliably localizes controlled execution changes that discrete pitch-mix and sequence representations cannot detect. These results demonstrate that UPG provides a traceable, multi-scale representation for identifying recurring strategy patterns without conflating retrospective associations with causal or future-performance claims.

</details>


### [83] [GRASP: Graph-Retrieval Automated Scoring Pipeline for Label-Free Multi-Topic Essay Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03857)
*Aafreen Husain,Samar Shailendra,Saad Sajid Hashmi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出GRASP（Graph-Retrieval Automated Scoring Pipeline），一种用于自动评分无标签多主题科学考试简答题的流水线。该方法利用Sentence-BERT和FAISS构建参考答案语义索引，通过图检索增强生成（GRAG）扩展直接余弦匹配，结合句数启发式和LLM判断答案段落包含的主题数量，最终用匈牙利算法将参考答案与段落片段最优匹配，并借助GPT-4.1-mini进行独立评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动简答评分研究聚焦于单一主题考试，而多主题考试（尤其是学生将多个题目答案混写在一个无标签段落中）的自动评分尚未充分探索。缺少题目与答案片段的对应关系，使得检索和评分变得困难。

Method: 1) 用Sentence-BERT将每道题的参考答案编码入FAISS向量索引；2) 在参考答案集合上构建语义相似度图；3) 通过句数启发式结合LLM预测学生文章中回答的主题数量；4) 通过余弦相似度RAG与图遍历GRAG检索候选参考节点；5) 用匈牙利算法将参考节点与每个问题片段最优一一匹配；6) 使用GPT-4.1-mini对每个匹配的片段独立评分。整个过程无需训练数据或领域特定示例。

Result: 实验验证了检索质量对评分准确性的影响，并展示了在不同段落复杂度水平上图增强检索（GRAG）相对于纯余弦相似度方法的优势。

Conclusion: GRASP证明了在无标签多主题科学考试场景中，结合语义图、检索增强生成和LLM评分的流水线能够有效实现自动评分，且图增强检索相比传统余弦相似度方法能提升检索覆盖率与最终评分准确性。

Abstract: Automated short-answer grading research has historically focused on exams consisting solely of questions pertaining to a single topic. Automatic grading of exams containing questions about more than one topic remains less explored. In this work, a Graph-Retrieval Automated Scoring Pipeline (GRASP) is introduced for grading label-free multi-topic science exams. Label-free exams are short-answer exams in which a student's responses to several distinct topics are merged into a single paragraph, with no markup labels or segmentation indicating which span answers which question. Reference answers for each question are encoded into a FAISS vector index via Sentence-BERT, and a semantic similarity graph is constructed over this set of reference answers. At grading time, sentence count heuristics, with a large language model used to resolve ambiguous cases, are first applied to predict how many distinct topics were answered in the student essay. This process is performed without training data or domain-specific example essays. Candidate reference nodes, each storing one (question, reference answer, concatenation of both) from the reference index, are then retrieved through cosine similarity based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GRAG). GRAG operates by taking the top cosine matches as seed nodes and then performing a graph traversal over strong edges to find additional reference nodes that may have been missed by RAG. The Hungarian algorithm is then used to optimally assign one reference node per question segment such that no reference is duplicated. Each segment is then graded against its assigned reference independently using GPT-4.1-mini. This experiment is performed to show the effect of retrieval quality on grading accuracy and the benefit of graph-augmented retrieval versus strict cosine similarity methods at various levels of essay complexity.

</details>


### [84] [Comparing Retrieval Methods for Academic Advisor Discovery: A Six-Method Study of 768 CS Faculty Profiles Across 9 US Universities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03901)
*Biraj Subedi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对六种信息检索方法在学术导师推荐任务上进行了比较评估，方法涵盖稀疏词法匹配（Jaccard、TF-IDF、BM25）、稠密语义检索（all-MiniLM-L6-v2）、混合分数融合及学习排序。基于新建的领域数据集（768份教师简介、162条分级相关性判断、5个查询），结果显示Reranked方法在NDCG@10上最优（0.477），TF-IDF显著最差；消融实验还发现仅用传记字段效果优于结合研究领域标签的完整模型，而拼接arXiv论文摘要会损害性能，提示应使用晚期融合架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为学术顾问发现这一特定任务提供系统化的检索方法比较，填补该领域缺乏基准数据集与严格统计评估的空白，并探索不同检索范式（稀疏、稠密、混合及学习排序）在此场景下的相对优劣，为实际部署提供参考依据。

Method: 构建了包含768份美国9个CS系教师简介的新领域数据集，由5个代表不同研究生研究画像的查询和162条0/1/2三级相关性判断构成。比较六种方法：Jaccard重叠、TF-IDF、BM25、all-MiniLM-L6-v2句子嵌入语义检索、混合分数融合及学习排序（Reranked）。使用Bonferroni校正的NDCG@10进行15对两两显著性检验，并实施字段消融实验（传记vs传记+研究标签）及受控实验（加入arXiv论文摘要的影响）。

Result: Reranked获得最高平均NDCG@10（0.477，标准差0.138），随后依次为Semantic（0.450）、Hybrid（0.421）、BM25（0.406）、Jaccard（0.303）和TF-IDF（0.246）。Bonferroni校正后仅TF-IDF显著差于BM25、Semantic、Hybrid和Reranked，其余配对差异不显著。字段消融显示仅用传记字段（NDCG 0.634）优于加入研究标签的完整模型（0.593）；拼接arXiv论文摘要使NDCG@10下降0.176，支持晚期融合设计。代码、爬虫和标注数据均公开。

Conclusion: 在学术导师发现任务中，重排序和学习排序方法表现最佳，朴素TF-IDF显著不足；传记字段本身即为最强信号，额外拼接长文本摘要反而引入噪声，最优做法是采用多源信号的晚期融合策略。该研究提供了开源基准，便于后续工作在此任务上进行比较和推进。

Abstract: We present a comparative evaluation of six information retrieval methods for the task of academic advisor discovery: ranking CS faculty members by relevance to a graduate applicant's research interest statement. The methods span sparse lexical matching (Jaccard overlap, TF-IDF, BM25), dense semantic retrieval (all-MiniLM-L6-v2 sentence embeddings), hybrid score fusion, and learning-to-rank. Evaluation uses a new domain-specific collection: 768 faculty profiles scraped from 9 US CS departments, with 162 graded relevance judgments (grade 0/1/2) across 5 queries representing distinct graduate student research profiles. Across all five queries, Reranked achieves the highest mean NDCG@10 (0.477, std 0.138), followed by Semantic (0.450), Hybrid (0.421), BM25 (0.406), Jaccard (0.303), and TF-IDF (0.246). After Bonferroni correction across all 15 pairwise comparisons, TF-IDF is significantly worse than BM25, Semantic, Hybrid, and Reranked; no other pairwise difference survives correction at 5 queries. A field ablation reveals that biography alone (NDCG 0.634) outperforms the full model combining biography with research area tags (0.593). A controlled experiment shows that concatenating arXiv paper abstracts reduces NDCG@10 by 0.176, motivating a late-fusion architecture. All code, scrapers, and relevance labels are released openly.

</details>


### [85] [The WebKurator.de Platform: Combined Regional and Topical Web Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03971)
*Michael Dinzinger,Natanael Arndt,Ben Böck,Jelena Mitrović,Michael Granitzer*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出WebKurator.de，一个面向德国网络的两维（主题+地理）协同网络策展平台，基于德国印迹数据集（554万网站）引导启动，其中约258万德国地址网站已完成主题标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国家图书馆和记忆机构在系统化策展网络内容方面面临挑战。现有目录式方法（如Curlie）采用以主题为中心的一维层级结构，地理方面被与主题和语言类别混织在一起，缺乏对区域相关内容的独立支持。

Method: 提出两维策展模型，显式分离主题分类与地理标注；平台集成基于LLM的主题分类、基于印迹（imprint）的地址提取与地理编码，并支持用户建议和审核；数据来源为德国印迹数据集（554万网站），从中提取并地理编码314万网站的邮政地址。

Result: 在314万含印迹页的网站地址中，258万（85.17%）网站位于德国并拥有主题标签，构成WebKurator.de的初始内容基础；平台可通过用户建议持续扩展。

Conclusion: WebKurator.de通过两维（主题×地理）策展模型及LLM自动化与人工审核结合的方式，为区域相关的网络内容策展提供了一种可行方案，可作为国家图书馆和记忆机构网络保存基础设施的增强工具。

Abstract: The systematic curation of the Web remains a central challenge for national libraries and memory institutions that aim to preserve culturally and regionally relevant content. Existing directory-based approaches such as Curlie implement a predominantly topic-centric, one-dimensional hierarchy, where geographic aspects are intertwined with topical and linguistic categories. To address this limitation, we present WebKurator.de, a collaborative platform for combined regional and topical web curation, initially focused on the German web. WebKurator introduces a two-dimensional curation model that explicitly separates topical categorization and geographic annotation. The system integrates LLM-based topic classification and imprint-based address extraction with geocoding, and supports user suggestions together with moderated review. The platform is bootstrapped from the German Imprints Dataset, a large-scale collection of 5.54 million websites. Among them, 3.14 million contain imprint pages, for which we successfully extracted and geocoded postal addresses. Of these, 2.58 million (85.17%) are located in Germany and also have an assigned topic label. These websites form the initial foundation of WebKurator.de and can be continuously extended through user suggestions.

</details>


### [86] [The Dice Roll Method: A Standardized Protocol for Repeated-Query Auditing of Large Language Model Brand Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04047)
*Dmitrij Żatuchin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出"骰子滚动法"(Dice Roll Method)作为LLM品牌推荐重复查询审计的标准化协议，通过对约19万条观测数据的再分析，给出探索性(n=5)、验证性(n=10)和严谨性(n=15)三档迭代次数指引，并通过39个独立语料单元中37个的预注册外部验证证实其可靠性预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者越来越多地使用重复相同提示来审计LLM品牌推荐中的随机变异，但缺乏标准化的协议来设定迭代次数、选择稳定性指标或建立信度阈值，导致研究结果难以比较和复现。

Method: 采用温度缩放核采样生成模型将总响应方差分解为采样、提示措辞、运行间和模型版本四个分量；构建负二项混合模型(以迭代作为重复测量)、Cliff's delta作为无分布效应量、依赖保持自助法、基于模拟的功效分析、概化理论分解、以及在固定快照上的漂移诊断。重新分析五项品牌推荐审计研究（约19万观测、270+品牌、6种语言、迭代次数5至40不等）。

Result: D-study得出三档迭代指引：探索性(n=5, G=0.58)、验证性(n=10, G=0.74)、严谨性(n=15, G=0.81)。四个度量族（计数、集合、嵌入、公平调整PASOR）具有互补性，支持紧凑度量组而非单一指标。预注册外部验证在39个单元中37个复现D-study可靠度预测，无失败案例，n=5的功效值精确到两位小数；但固定档位不能跨语料迁移，支持"先试点再求解"的解读。

Conclusion: 该协议为LLM品牌推荐的重复查询审计提供了统计原理基础，适用于真实自回归生成的条件性、非高斯结构。

Abstract: Background: Researchers increasingly use repeated identical prompts to audit stochastic variation in large language model (LLM) brand recommendations, yet no standardized protocol exists for setting iteration counts, selecting stability metrics, or establishing reliability thresholds. Objective: We formalize the Dice Roll Method as a reusable protocol for repeated-query auditing of LLM brand recommendations, grounded in a generative model of temperature-scaled nucleus sampling. Methods: Total response variance is decomposed into sampling, prompt-phrasing, run-to-run, and model-version components. The stack: a negative-binomial mixed model with iterations as repeated measures; Cliff's delta as the distribution-free effect size; dependence-preserving bootstrap; simulation-based power; a generalizability-theory decomposition; drift diagnostics on pinned snapshots. We reanalyse five brand-recommendation auditing studies: approximately 190,000 observations, 270+ brands, 6 languages, iteration counts 5 to 40. Results: Three tiers of iteration guidance emerge from the D-study: exploratory (n = 5, G = 0.58), confirmatory (n = 10, G = 0.74), and rigorous (n = 15, G = 0.81), tied to effect-size and generalizability targets. The four metric families (count, set, embedding, fairness-adjusted PASOR) are complementary, motivating a compact metric battery over single indicators. A pre-registered external validation on three independent corpora (Motoki et al., 100-round; Rozado, 24 models; llm-stability) reproduces the D-study reliability prediction in 37 of 39 cells with no failures and the n = 5 power value to two decimals; the fixed tiers do not transfer, supporting a pilot-then-solve reading. Conclusion: The protocol gives repeated-query auditing of LLM brand recommendations a statistically principled footing under the conditional, non-Gaussian structure of real autoregressive generation.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [87] [Requirements After the First Edit: Mining Late Requirement Emergence and Rework in Real-World Coding-Agent Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03028)
*Bowen Jiang,Haowei Cheng,Yuhong Fu,Anne Koziolek,Jialong Li,Weixing Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析3,553个SWE-chat会话，量化了编码代理场景中后期需求（post-implementation requirement arrivals）导致的代码失效（invalidation）现象。研究发现，需求的到达伴随的代码删除或替换（即失效代理）是非需求编辑的两倍左右，且该负担在整个会话中无明显下降趋势。受控实验表明，延迟披露会将实现工作推迟到需求揭示之后，而预先警告对覆盖行为无显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理常在用户尚未充分明确需求时就实施更改，这呼应了需求工程中的经典问题：利益相关者在部分系统存在之前无法表达约束。传统软件工程在发布周期粒度上研究需求动荡带来的超支风险，但现有编码代理研究仅部分弥合该差距：基准测试刻意在实现前固定需求，观测研究则仅报告拒绝频率而未将需求到达与代码失效直接关联。

Method: 利用3,553个合格的SWE-chat会话，沿三个维度对实现后的需求到达进行编码，并在可回放仓库状态的情况下，将每次需求到达与一个代理指标关联——即先前代理编写代码行的删除或替换。作者进行了鲁棒性检查（用户轮次和净删除检查），并开展了受控实验以检验信息披露时机（延迟披露 vs 预先警告）对实现后覆盖的影响。

Result: 需求到达后的代码失效约为匹配的非需求编辑的两倍（该结果经用户轮次和净删除检查后保持稳健，但未证明因果关系）。该负担在会话过程中无显著下降趋势，且在校正多重比较后未发现与操作类型的显著关联，若干置信区间较宽。受控实验显示，延迟披露会将实现转移到揭示之后，而预先警告对覆盖行为无检测到的影响。

Conclusion: 晚期需求涌现是编码代理场景中一个可测量的代码失效来源。研究建立了需求到达与代码失效之间的量化联系，为后续在编码代理设计中缓解需求动荡的影响提供了实证基础，但因果性尚未确立，部分效应区间仍存在不确定性。

Abstract: Coding agents often implement changes before users have fully articulated their requirements, echoing a pattern from requirements engineering: stakeholders cannot express a constraint until part of the system exists to react to. This volatility is associated with schedule and budget overruns in traditional projects, but only at release-cycle granularity. Existing work on coding agents narrows this gap only partway: curated benchmarks fix requirements before implementation by design, and observational studies report pushback frequency without linking arrivals to the code invalidation they cause. We address this using 3,553 eligible SWE-chat sessions, coding post-implementation requirement arrivals along three dimensions and, where repository state can be replayed, linking each arrival to a proxy: deletion or replacement of prior agent-authored lines. A requirement's arrival is followed by roughly twice as much invalidation as matched non-requirement edits, robust to user-turn and net-deletion checks, though not demonstrated as causal. This burden shows no detectable decline over a session and no detected association with operation type once multiplicity is accounted for; several intervals remain wide. A controlled experiment shows delayed disclosure relocates implementation post-reveal, while advance warning produces no detected effect on overwriting. These results establish late requirement emergence as a measurable source of code invalidation.

</details>


### [88] [Large Language Models and Language Server Protocol: a match made in context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03086)
*Alessandro Schena,Ilgiz Mustafin,Julia Kotovich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍Eiffel-tools，一个基于语言服务器协议（LSP）的Eiffel编程语言工具，利用大型语言模型（LLMs）辅助开发经过静态验证的软件。该工具结合LLM生成和静态验证器验证，通过迭代重试机制修复代码缺陷，在2个公开数据集上使用3种模型进行评估，可修复76%-95%的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决LLM在生成静态验证软件时输出不可靠的问题，通过将LLM与形式化验证器结合，为Eiffel语言开发者提供交互式和非交互式的代码及规范生成辅助，确保生成代码满足验证要求。

Method: 实现LSP协议的工具，利用语言和项目特定知识构造丰富的程序化提示（prompt）指导LLM生成代码和规范；使用静态验证器验证LLM输出，对不合格的输出进行纠正或拒绝，并迭代重试直至程序通过验证。通过结合LLM（3种模型）与形式化验证器，在2个公共数据集上评估缺陷修复能力。

Result: 该工具结合LLM和形式化验证器可修复76%至95%的缺陷（取决于所用模型和提示策略）。研究结果揭示了修复尝试次数与成功率之间存在权衡关系。

Conclusion: LLM与静态验证器结合能显著提升自动缺陷修复的成功率，但需要在实际应用中平衡尝试次数与修复效果之间的资源消耗。

Abstract: This article introduces Eiffel-tools, a language server protocol (LSP) implementation for the Eiffel programming language that uses Large Language Models (LLMs) to aid the development of statically verified software. The tool provides various interactive and non-interactive commands to produce code and specifications. It uses language and project specific knowledge to precisely direct the LLM and verifies the output using a static verifier. It crafts rich programmatic prompts for the input and corrects or rejects the output. Furthermore, it handles the retries until the program passes verification. The tool's bug fixing capability is evaluated on 2 public datasets using 3 models. The tool can fix 76% to 95% of bugs by combining LLMs and a formal verifier depending on the model and prompts used. The results show the trade-off between the number of fixing attempts and the success rate.

</details>


### [89] [Compound Prompt Constraints in LLM Code Generation: A Factorial Study of Format, Persona, and Urgency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03156)
*Shrenik Jadhav,Nickalsa LaPlaca,Caleb Stone,Ashok Raja,Omar Ochoa,Vidhyashree Nagaraju*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过全因子实验（3x3x3=27种组合）系统研究了输出格式、人设分配和紧迫性框架三个因素如何共同影响LLM代码生成的可靠性。使用5个OpenAI模型（GPT-4o家族、GPT-4.1家族和o3-mini）在全部164个HumanEval+问题上进行了22,140次贪心解码评估，发现复合约束会产生架构依赖性的超加性退化，其中GPT-4o家族表现出一致的超加性效应（pass@1下降3-12个百分点），而GPT-4.1家族基本免疫，o3-mini则呈现不同模式——结构化输出甚至能提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在软件工程代码生成流水线中越来越普及，而实际生产提示词通常同时包含多个约束条件（如输出格式、人设、紧迫性）。然而，以往研究大多只考察单一约束因素的影响，对多个约束条件如何联合（而非仅仅加性地）影响LLM可靠性这一问题存在认知空白。作者希望通过受控的全因子实验来揭示单一因素实验无法预测的复合约束效应。

Method: 采用3x3x3全因子受控实验设计，自变量包括：输出格式（如JSON/XML等）、人设分配（如专家人设等）和紧迫性框架（如中等/高紧迫性等），共27种条件组合。将每种复合条件分解为加性预测与残差交互项，以捕捉超加性退化。使用了5个OpenAI模型（GPT-4o家族、GPT-4.1家族、o3-mini）和全部164个HumanEval+问题，共22,140次贪心解码评估。采用格式感知的提取管道区分格式失败与推理失败，并以McNemar检验、比值比和95%置信区间进行显著性评估。

Result: 研究发现复合约束可产生架构依赖性退化，这种退化无法从单因素实验中预测。GPT-4o家族表现出一致的超加性效应，pass@1比加性预测低3-12个百分点；最大交互效应为GPT-4o-mini上JSON+专家人设+中等紧迫性组合导致的-12.2个百分点。JSON组合通常比XML产生更大的交互效应。相比之下，GPT-4.1家族对复合约束基本不敏感，而o3-mini展现不同的定性模式——结构化输出约束反而能改善性能。

Conclusion: 脆弱性是架构依赖的而非规模依赖的；单个中性甚至有益的约束组合起来可导致显著退化。因此，复合提示词测试应成为LLM辅助工程流水线可靠性评估的标准做法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in software engineering pipelines for code generation, where production prompts often combine multiple constraints. This paper presents a full-factorial empirical study of how output formatting, persona assignment, and urgency framing jointly affect LLM code-generation reliability. We evaluate all 27 combinations in a controlled 3x3x3 design and decompose each compound condition into an additive prediction and a residual interaction term that captures super-additive degradation. The study uses all 164 HumanEval+ problems across five OpenAI models from the GPT-4o family, GPT-4.1 family, and o3-mini, yielding 22,140 greedy-decoding evaluations. A format-aware extraction pipeline separates formatting failures from reasoning failures, and significance is assessed with McNemar's test, odds ratios, and 95% confidence intervals.
  Results show that compound constraints can produce architecture-dependent degradation not predictable from single-factor experiments. The GPT-4o family exhibits consistent super-additive effects, with pass@1 reductions 3-12 percentage points beyond additive predictions; the largest interaction is -12.2 pp on GPT-4o-mini for JSON + expert persona + moderate urgency. JSON combinations generally produce larger interactions than XML. In contrast, the GPT-4.1 family is largely resistant, while o3-mini shows a qualitatively different pattern in which structured output constraints can improve performance. These findings show that vulnerability is architecture-dependent rather than size-dependent, that individually neutral or beneficial constraints can combine to cause substantial degradation, and that compound-prompt testing should be standard in reliability assessment for LLM-assisted engineering pipelines.

</details>


### [90] [Two Truths and A Lie? Benchmarking Off-the-Shelf LLMs for Requirements Quality Assessment: Performance, False Alarms, and Misses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03230)
*Jannatul Shefa,Alejandro Salado,Paul Wach,Taylan G. Topcu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本论文首次对现成大语言模型（LLM）在需求质量评估任务中的表现进行了基准测试分析。基于INCOSE质量标准构建专家级金标准，评估了来自OpenAI和Anthropic两个家族共十个模型，发现模型对专家识别问题的中位检测率仅为47%，误报率为11%，且在需要系统工程判断的问题（如必要性、正确性）上几乎完全失效，表明现成LLM尚不能作为可信的自主需求质量评估器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求工程（RE）决定了系统工程下游一切环节的质量，有缺陷的需求经过评审后会传播为设计返工、进度延误和成本超支。由于需求通常以自然语言书写，生成式AI的最新进展使人们期待LLM能够承担需求质量评估这一缓慢且高度依赖人类专家的任务，但关于LLM能否被信任承担该任务的实证证据仍然匮乏。

Method: 研究基于INCOSE质量标准构建专家派生的金标准，对跨越OpenAI和Anthropic两个模型家族、各自五代的共十个现成模型进行评估。实验覆盖100次独立运行、两组需求数据集和五种采样温度，对LLM的需求质量评估性能进行系统化基准测试。

Result: 研究发现四个关键结果：(1) 错误分布高度不对称——最优Anthropic模型对专家识别的问题中位检测率仅47%，误报率为11%；(2) 在需要系统工程判断的场景下（必要性、正确性类问题）性能严重退化，几乎总是被遗漏；(3) 代际进步是非单调的，新版模型不一定优于旧版；(4) 误差行为随采样温度变化仅呈现温和且非单调的波动，说明缺陷源于模型固有特征而非随机性。现成LLM尚不可用作可信的自主评估器，且在Agentic AI架构中编排这些模块可能加剧而非纠正其缺陷。

Conclusion: 现成LLM目前不适合作为自主的需求质量评估器，其短期内合理的定位是在人机回环（human-in-the-loop）中充当决策支持工具。研究同时提醒Agentic AI开发者，将这些LLM模块编排进专业架构可能复合而非纠正其系统性缺陷。

Abstract: Requirements engineering (RE) governs the quality of everything downstream in systems engineering (SE); defective requirements that survive review cycles propagate into design rework, schedule delays, and cost overruns. Because requirements are often written in natural language, recent advances in generative AI have raised expectations that large language models (LLMs) can absorb requirement quality assessment, a task otherwise slow and human expertise-intensive. Yet empirical evidence on whether LLMs can be trusted to do so remains scarce. This study presents the first benchmarking analysis of off-the-shelf LLM performance for requirement quality evaluation. Against an expert-derived ground truth built on INCOSE quality criteria, we evaluate ten models spanning two families (OpenAI and Anthropic) and five generations each, across one hundred independent runs, two requirement sets, and five sampling temperatures. Four contributions follow. First, we quantify a strongly asymmetric error profile: across all models and runs, the best-performing Anthropic model detects a median of only 47% of expert-identified issues while false-flagging 11%. Second, performance degrades significantly where SE judgment is required, as necessity and correctness issues are almost always missed. Third, generational progress is non-monotonic, so newer models cannot be assumed better. Fourth, this error behavior shifts only modestly and non-monotonically across sampling temperatures, indicating characteristic model deficiencies rather than inherent stochasticity. Off-the-shelf LLMs are therefore not yet trustworthy autonomous evaluators. Findings also warrant caution for Agentic AI developers: orchestrating these LLM modules in specialized architectures risks compounding these deficiencies rather than correcting them. Their defensible near-term role is human-in-the-loop decision support.

</details>


### [91] [Refusing the Impossible: A Taxonomy and Benchmark for Code Hallucination in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03267)
*Vishnu Asutosh Dasu,Ashish Kundu,Gang Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大型语言模型（LLM）的"代码幻觉"问题——即LLM生成的代码看似合理但缺乏现实基础（如导入不存在的包或声称实现违反已证定理的算法）。作者提出一个三维分类体系（基础性、表现层面、行为），构建了一个对抗性测试套件（270个不可满足的提示，跨6种语言和24个子类别，配91个可解对照），并基于12个开源代码/推理模型的4,332个评判响应发现：模型在约60%的不可满足提示上生成无基础代码，仅27%会拒绝，但对可解对照的误拒绝率为0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码经常看似合理但缺乏现实基础——例如导入不存在的包或声称实现违反已验证定理的算法，但代码仍能编译运行。现有研究通常将所有代码错误混为一谈，缺乏将"代码幻觉"（无基础生成）与普通"代码错误"（有基础程序中的bug）区分开的系统性分析框架。作者旨在提供一个清晰的分类法和一个可量化的对抗性评估套件，以更好地理解和衡量这一问题。

Method: 论文提出：(1) 一个三维分类体系，包括基础性（绝对违反普遍真理性事实 vs. 相对捏造的情境/生态系统特定事实）、表现层面（句法、语义或事实层面）、行为（从自信捏造到退化输出），并组织成严重性排序；(2) 一个对抗性评测套件，包含270个故意不可满足的提示（跨6种语言、24个子类别），配91个可解对照用于校准；(3) 两层级评判协议，经人类标注验证（82%一致率，κ=0.73）。评测12个开源权重代码和推理模型，共4,332个评判响应。

Result: 在12个开源代码/推理模型的4,332个评判响应中：模型在约60%的不可满足提示上生成无基础（幻觉）代码；仅27%的提示被正确拒绝；对可解对照的误拒绝率为0%（表明模型的拒绝行为是区分性的，不会过度拒绝合法请求）。两层级评判协议与人工标注有82%的一致性和κ=0.73的Cohen's Kappa。

Conclusion: LLM在遇到真正不可满足的编码任务时存在严重的"代码幻觉"问题——大多数模型倾向于生成看似合理但实际无基础的代码而非拒绝作答。模型的拒绝能力虽然精准（不会误拒绝可解问题），但覆盖率极低（仅27%）。该分类体系和对抗性评测套件为理解和改进LLM在编码任务中的真实性提供了一个系统化工具，有助于推动更可靠的代码生成。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often produce code that looks plausible but is not grounded in reality. The code may import packages that do not exist or claim to implement algorithms that violate proven theorems, while still compiling and running. We study \emph{code hallucination} as \emph{ungrounded generation} and separate it from ordinary \emph{code error} (bugs in otherwise grounded programs). We propose a taxonomy with three dimensions: \textbf{groundedness} (absolute violations of universal truths vs.\ relative fabrications of contingent or ecosystem-specific facts), \textbf{manifestation level} (syntactic, semantic, or factual), and \textbf{behavior} (from confident fabrication to degenerate output), organized into a severity ordering. We build an \textbf{adversarial} suite of deliberately unsatisfiable tasks where the correct response is to refuse and categorize the responses under our taxonomy. The suite contains \textbf{270 prompts} across six languages and 24 subcategories, paired with \textbf{91 matched solvable controls}, and responses are judged by a two-tier protocol validated against human labels (82\% agreement, $κ{=}0.73$). Across twelve open-weight code and reasoning models (4{,}332 judged responses), models produce ungrounded code on about 60\% of unsatisfiable prompts and refuse only 27\%, while wrongly refusing 0\% of the solvable controls.

</details>


### [92] [TIPCODER: Reinforcement Learning Boosted Test-time Instruction Proposer for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03309)
*Minyu Chen,Sihao Wu,Ling-I Wu,Song Qin,Jingyang Li,Lei Ning,Jianxin Xue,Guoqiang Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TipCoder是一种测试时指令提案器，通过生成问题特定的辅助提示（tips）来进行实例级指令空间探索，用于代码生成。它蒸馏多轮调试轨迹为主动指导，并用边际效用奖励进行强化学习优化，推理时生成基础解和提示引导解并通过奖励模型进行后选择，提供一致的指令级测试时扩展策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的测试时扩展（test-time scaling）仅通过从固定指令中采样多个程序来探索解空间，忽略了指令空间层面的探索。许多代码生成失败源于原始提示中缺失的约束、被忽视的边界情况或误导性的推理路径，因此需要一种在指令层面进行补充和改进的机制。

Method: TipCoder包含三个核心组件：(1) 指令提案器（Proposer），从多轮调试轨迹中蒸馏出问题特定的主动引导提示（tips）；(2) 使用边际效用奖励（marginal-utility reward）的强化学习来优化Proposer；(3) 推理时生成基础解和tips引导的解，通过奖励模型（Reward Model）进行后期选择，构成探索-选择设计。

Result: 在多个代码生成基准和多个目标Code LLM上，TipCoder提供了稳定一致的指令级测试时扩展策略，在共享的基于奖励模型的选择协议下，优于随机采样和通用提示优化的基准方法。

Conclusion: 指令级探索是补充解空间采样（如随机采样）的有效正交方向。TipCoder的探索-选择设计既能通过tips发掘额外的候选潜力，又能通过奖励模型选择和边际效用奖励减少不必要引导带来的回归，为代码生成的测试时扩展提供了新范式。

Abstract: Test-time scaling for code generation typically explores the solution space by sampling multiple programs from a fixed instruction. We study a complementary direction: instance-level instruction-space exploration. Our observation is that many coding failures stem from missing constraints, overlooked edge cases, or misleading reasoning paths induced by the original prompt. To address this, we propose TipCoder, a test-time instruction proposer that generates problem-specific auxiliary tips before code synthesis. TipCoder distills multi-turn debugging trajectories into proactive guidance and further optimizes the Proposer with reinforcement learning using a marginal-utility reward. At inference time, it generates both a base solution and a tip-guided solution, and applies a Reward Model for post-hoc selection. This exploration-selection design allows tips to expose additional candidate potential while reducing regressions from unnecessary guidance. Across the evaluated code-generation benchmarks and target Code LLMs, TipCoder provides a consistent instruction-level test-time scaling strategy, comparing favorably with stochastic sampling and generic prompt optimization baselines under a shared reward-model-based selection protocol.

</details>


### [93] [The Psychological Costs of Artificial Intelligence Adoption in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03456)
*Adam Alami,Elda Paja,Abhishek Tiwari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过在某大型软件开发服务公司的案例研究（N=21次半结构化访谈），揭示软件专业人员在组织引入AI一年后面临的心理成本，包括问责焦虑、工艺身份断裂、意义与满意度侵蚀、认知与工作量强化以及不确定性困扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织在AI用于软件工程时往往聚焦于生产力等有形收益，忽略了AI作为颠覆性力量进入成熟工作环境可能带来的心理和社会压力。历史上技术颠覆常致焦虑、技能退化与职业身份危机，因此AI对软件工程师的心理成本需要被系统理解。

Method: 在某大型软件服务公司AI采用一年后进行案例研究，通过会议参与和21次半结构化访谈收集定性数据，对软件专业人员经历的AI采用心理成本进行主题分析。

Result: 识别出五类心理成本：问责焦虑、工艺身份断裂、意义与满意度侵蚀、认知与工作量强化、不确定性困扰。从业人员通过恢复控制的实践来管理、通过保护性和身份保持性适应来缓解，或无可奈何时选择吸收这些成本。

Conclusion: 应将AI采用重新定位为一种"人的转变"（human transition），而非仅是技术与组织的转变，为SE中AI-人类协作研究提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly used to augment software engineering (SE) workflows. While code generation remains the main use case, organizations are actively seeking AI integration in other practices such as test cases generation and code reviews. Organizational AI adoption strategies seem to focus on tangible outcomes such as productivity. However, AI is a disruptive force, introduced into settings where role identity, team norms, and the sources of job satisfaction were well established before the recent advances in generative AI. Historically, technological disruptions have caused psychological and social strains in workplaces, ranging from anxiety and eroded meaning to deskilling and disrupted professional identities. The assumption that AI for SE is cost-free may not be accurate. Therefore, in this study we sought to understand the psychological costs software professionals experience during organizational AI adoption. We carried out a case study in a large software development services company, one year after the company launched its AI adoption. We collected qualitative data through meetings and semi-structured interviews (N = 21). We found that software professionals experience accountability anxiety, craft identity disruption, meaning and satisfaction erosion, cognitive and workload intensification, and uncertainty distress. Practitioners manage these costs through practices that restore control, mitigate them through protective and identity-preserving adaptations, or absorb them, carrying what neither can resolve. We contribute to AI-human collaboration in SE by repositioning AI adoption as a human transition, not only a technological and organizational one.

</details>


### [94] [Code Transformation Rule Synthesis using LLMs: Potential and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03592)
*Axel Allain,Aymeric Blot,Djamel Eddine Khelladi,Mathieu Acher*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对三个领域特定语言（Comby、GritQL、Ast-Grep）进行实证研究，评估三个LLM在四个软件演化任务（API误用修正、程序修复、API迁移、语言版本迁移）上合成转换规则的能力。结果表明GPT-5.4在规则应用率和接近真实变换方面表现最佳，小型开源模型对简单局部改动有效但复杂迁移场景吃力，LLM整体在正确性上优于anti-unification算法但在规则可应用性上有所不及。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的黑箱特性导致可解释性有限和确定性不足，且在大规模代码库上的重复性任务使用成本高昂。为缓解这些问题，作者探索用领域特定语言（DSL）将LLM的深层知识转化为可复用的代码转换规则，以实现更高效、可解释的代码变换。

Method: 选取三种DSL（Comby、GritQL、Ast-Grep），评估三个LLM（GPT-5.4、GPT-oss-120B、Llama3.1-8B），在六个多样化数据集上覆盖四类软件演化任务（API误用修正、程序修复、API迁移、语言版本迁移），并与anti-unification算法对比规则正确性与可应用性。

Result: GPT-5.4规则应用率持续较高，且在大多数基准上产生的变换最接近真值。GPT-oss-120B和Llama3.1-8B对简单局部改动有效，但在复杂迁移场景中表现挣扎。通过元变量使用和首个四分位数中的高复用分数观察到不可忽略的泛化能力。与anti-unification算法相比，LLM在正确性上胜出但在规则可应用性上不及。

Conclusion: 转换规则合成已超越概念验证阶段，LLM在生成合理、正确、可泛化且可复用的规则方面展现出巨大潜力。这为利用LLM知识生成可解释规则、降低重复性大规模代码库任务成本提供了有力证据。

Abstract: Due to their black-box nature, LLMs suffer from limited explain- ability and a lack of determinism. Their usage cost can also rise, particularly with repetitive tasks on large codebases. To mitigate this, we conduct a novel empirical study targeting three domain- specific languages for transformation rules, namely Comby, GritQL, and Ast-Grep. We evaluate three LLMs (GPT-5.4, GPT-oss-120B, and Llama3.1-8B) on six diverse datasets covering four software- evolution tasks: API misuse correction, program repair, API migra- tion, and language version migration. Our results provide evidence that transformation rule synthesis moves beyond proof-of-concept with strong frontier models. GPT-5.4 achieves consistently high rule applicability rates and produces transformations closest to the ground truth across most benchmarks. Smaller and open-weight GPT-oss-120B and Llama3.1-8B models remain effective for simpler, localized changes but struggle with complex migration scenarios. We also observe non-negligible generalizability through the usage of meta-variables and through a high reuse score in the first quartile of many datasets. Finally, when compared to the anti-unification algorithm, LLMs outperform it in correctness, but underperform in rule applicability. Overall, our results show great potential for LLMs to generate sound, correct, generalizable, and reusable rules.

</details>


### [95] [Can LLMs Extract Architectural Design Decisions from Source Code Commits? - A Preliminary Exploratory Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03721)
*Amey Karan,Rudra Dhar,Mohamed Soliman,Karthik Vaidhyanathan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一项初步研究，旨在探索利用四种大语言模型（Gemini 3 Pro、DeepSeek R1、Kimi K2、Qwen3）从源代码提交中自动恢复架构设计决策（ADDs），采用零样本和少样本提示策略，在30条开发者书写的ADD上进行评估。结果表明所有模型BERT-F1分数均超过0.81，少样本提示能提升对齐度，但生成的ADDs往往过长、偏重实现细节，且缺失决策背后的基本原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 架构设计决策（ADDs）记录了软件系统结构和演化背后的基本原理，但很少被显式文档化，通常隐藏在源码提交中。从提交中恢复ADDs对架构知识管理（AKM）至关重要，但由于ADDs的隐式和非结构化特性，提取极具挑战。尽管LLM在理解代码和文本方面表现出色，其在该任务上的有效性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 选取四种LLM（Gemini 3 Pro、DeepSeek R1、Kimi K2、Qwen3），采用零样本和少样本两种提示方式，在来自开源项目的30条开发者撰写的ADDs上进行实验。使用ROUGE-L、BLEU、METEOR和BERTScore四种自动评估指标对输出打分，并由一位作者对Gemini模型的输出进行人工审阅。

Result: 所有模型均达到0.81以上的BERT-F1分数；少样本提示提升了与参考ADDs的对齐度（Gemini的BERT-F1从0.828提升至0.847）。然而，生成的ADDs普遍过长、过于关注实现层面，并且遗漏了隐藏在决策背后的基本原理/理由。

Conclusion: 研究表明LLM具备从代码提交中恢复ADDs的基本能力，但现有方法仍有明显局限。这为架构感知的LLM系统（architecture-aware LLM systems）以及自动化架构知识管理（AKM）指出了改进方向和发展机遇。

Abstract: Context: Architectural Design Decisions (ADDs) capture the rationale behind the structure and evolution of software systems but are rarely documented explicitly, and are often hidden inside source code commits. Recovering them is important for Architectural Knowledge Management (AKM). Problem: Extracting ADDs from commits is challenging due to their implicit and unstructured nature. Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in understanding code and text, yet their effectiveness for this task remains underexplored. Study: We present a preliminary study using four LLMs (Gemini 3 Pro, DeepSeek R1, Kimi K2, Qwen3) with zeroshot and fewshot prompting on 30 developer-written ADDs from open-source projects. We score outputs with ROUGE-L, BLEU, METEOR, and BERTScore, and one author manually reviews the Gemini outputs. Results: All models reach a BERT-F1 above 0.81, and fewshot prompting improves alignment (Gemini BERT-F1: 0.828 to 0.847). However, the generated ADDs are often too long, implementation-focused, and miss the rationale behind the decision. This highlights opportunities for architecture-aware LLM systems and automated AKM.

</details>


### [96] [No One Left Behind: Cross-Level Analysis for Sustainable Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03861)
*Masoum Salehi,Sandro Schulze,Jacob Krüger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了"可持续性反模式"（sustainability antipatterns）的概念，用于捕获软件工程中跨社会技术层面系统性产生不可持续结果的条件，并揭示组织、流程和技术层面之间的错位（misalignments）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件可持续性研究（如能效、资源优化、代码可维护性）对可持续性问题如何产生并在社会技术各层面间相互强化缺乏充分解释。作者认为可持续性挑战并非孤立存在，而是系统性的，源于组织优先级、开发实践和技术条件之间的交互作用，需要一种跨层面、跨维度的分析视角。

Method: 这是一篇愿景论文（vision paper），通过概念性的方式提出"可持续性反模式"作为分析工具，用以暴露社会技术层面与可持续性维度之间难以通过以工件为中心或以单一维度为中心的分析所识别的错位，并勾勒出识别、形式化、检测和缓解此类反模式的研究方向。

Result: 本文未提供实证结果，而是提出一个概念框架和研究议程：将可持续性反模式作为基础，构建一个集成且可操作的可持续软件工程研究方向，涵盖识别、形式化、检测和缓解四个具体维度。

Conclusion: 可持续性反模式有望成为理解跨层面、跨维度系统性可持续性问题的基础工具，为可持续软件工程提供集成化的行动研究议程。现有的以单维度和工件为中心的分析方法不足以揭示此类系统性问题。

Abstract: Software engineers expect a software system to be efficient, maintainable, economically viable, and socially responsible throughout its lifecycle. Unfortunately, decisions made at the organizational, process, or product levels of software development - even when they improve one of these goals - often have unintended long-term consequences at other levels. In response, software-engineering research has explored different ways to achieve software sustainability, including energy efficiency, resource optimization, and code maintainability. However, existing research offers limited explanations for how sustainability problems emerge and reinforce one another across the socio-technical levels of software engineering. Moreover, we argue that sustainability challenges are not isolated but rather systemic: They stem from interactions among organizational priorities, development practices, and technical conditions. In this vision paper, we introduce the concept of sustainability antipatterns to capture conditions that systematically produce unsustainable outcomes. We demonstrate how this concept can help expose cross-level misalignments between socio-technical levels and sustainability dimensions that remain difficult to recognize through artifact-centric or dimension-specific analyses. To guide future research, we outline concrete directions for identifying, formalizing, detecting, and mitigating sustainability anti-patterns. We envision sustainability anti-patterns as a foundation for an integrated and actionable research agenda on sustainable software engineering.

</details>


### [97] [LabelMate: An LLM-Driven Framework for Refined Issue Report Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04055)
*Liam Johnston,Shayan Noei,Maram Assi,Ying Zou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出LabelMate，一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的框架，能够从历史issue报告中自动推导项目特定的标签集，并自动为新issue报告分配标签，无需任何预标注训练数据。在30个GitHub仓库的16,500份issue报告上评估，生成了275个连贯标签，平均标注准确率达89.84%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件issue报告标注对有效规划和社区参与至关重要，但许多issue报告因人工设计标签体系及分配标签成本高昂而未被标注。现有自动化标注方法存在关键局限：大量人工干预、只能分配通用标签、依赖已有标注数据集。

Method: 提出LabelMate框架：(1) 利用LLM从历史issue报告中推导全面的项目特定标签集；(2) 自动为新的issue报告分配相关标签，整个过程无需任何预标注训练数据。

Result: 在来自30个流行且多样化的GitHub仓库的16,500份issue报告上进行评估，生成275个连贯标签列表，平均标注准确率89.84%，相比现有通用标签分配方法有统计学显著提升。

Conclusion: LabelMate提供了一种高效、领域自适应的解决方案，可简化issue标注流程，克服了现有方法需要人工干预、通用标签和依赖标注数据的局限性。

Abstract: Software users often submit issue reports to a product's issue tracking system to report defects, suggest enhancements, or raise other product-related concerns. Labeling these issue reports supports effective planning and improves community engagement. However, many issue reports remain unlabeled due to the substantial manual effort required to design an appropriate label taxonomy, then assign suitable labels from this taxonomy to new issue reports. Existing automated labeling approaches attempt to mitigate these challenges. However, they suffer from key limitations, such as extensive manual intervention, the assignment of generic labels, and a dependence on existing labeled datasets. To address these limitations, we propose LabelMate, a novel Large Language Model (LLM)-driven framework that (1) derives a comprehensive, project-specific label set from historical issue reports and (2) automatically assigns relevant labels to new issue reports without requiring any pre-labeled training data. We evaluate LabelMate on 16,500 issue reports from 30 popular and diverse GitHub repositories. Based on this dataset, our approach generates a coherent list of 275 labels and achieves an average labeling accuracy of 89.84%, a statistically significant improvement over existing generic label assigning approaches. These results demonstrate that LabelMate offers an efficient, domain-adaptive solution to streamline the issue labeling process.

</details>


### [98] [When Models Edit Too Much: On the Fidelity of Minimal Code Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04061)
*Tongyao Zhu,Wei Hern Lim,Min-Yen Kan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM代码修复中的过度编辑问题，构建了一个基于400个BigCodeBench问题的AST级损坏评估框架，发现GPT-5.5等前沿模型普遍存在过度编辑现象。通过保留指令可将平均多余Levenshtein距离从0.195降至0.131，额外认知复杂度减少26.6%，Pass@1提升2.3点。研究还发现强化学习相比监督微调在修复保真度和性能保持的平衡上表现更优，证明编辑保真度是可测量且可学习的独立代码修复质量维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码编辑评估仅关注正确性，忽略了修复应具备的最小性、可审查性和对原始实现的忠实度。过度编辑（模型超出修复bug所需范围重写代码的倾向）被广泛忽视，本研究旨在系统性地量化和缓解这一问题。

Method: 从BigCodeBench选取400个问题，通过在参考解决方案中注入受控的AST级损坏来构建评估框架，确保每个修复任务都有已知的最小修补方案。对前沿LLM进行过度编辑测试，评估清除指令的影响，并对比监督微调与强化学习在提升编辑保真度方面的效果。

Result: 过度编辑在包括GPT-5.5在内的前沿模型中广泛存在；高Pass@1可与不必要的过大编辑和增加的认知复杂度共存。保留指令能显著降低过度编辑（多余Levenshtein距离从0.195降至0.131，额外认知复杂度降26.6%，Pass@1提高2.3点），但这些改进并非源于更大的推理预算或更大模型。监督微调会过拟合到已见过的损坏模式，而强化学习在域外编辑保真度和性能保持之间取得了最佳平衡。

Conclusion: 编辑保真度是代码修复质量的一个独立且重要的衡量维度，可以通过指令引导和强化学习等方法进行测量和提升。单纯增大模型或推理预算不能有效缓解过度编辑，需要有针对性的训练策略。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to edit existing code, but correctness alone is not enough: useful repairs should also be minimal, reviewable, and faithful to the original implementation. We study over-editing, the tendency of a model to rewrite code beyond what is required to fix a bug. We construct an evaluation framework from 400 BigCodeBench problems by injecting controlled AST-level corruptions into reference solutions, giving each repair task a known minimal patch. Across frontier LLMs, over-editing is widespread even among strong models like GPT-5.5: high Pass@1 can coexist with unnecessarily large edits and added cognitive complexity. A preservation instruction substantially reduces this behavior, lowering average excess Levenshtein distance from 0.195 to 0.131, reducing added cognitive complexity by 26.6%, and increasing Pass@1 by 2.3 points. However, these gains do not simply follow from a larger reasoning budget or larger models. We next ask whether minimal editing can be learned directly during post-training. We observe that supervised fine-tuning overfits to seen corruption patterns, whereas reinforcement learning gives the best out-of-domain edit-fidelity and performance-retention trade-off. These results position edit fidelity as a distinct axis of code-repair quality and show that it can be measured and learned.

</details>


### [99] [ATIBA: Grounded Integrity and Quality Checking for Research Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04123)
*Veli Karakaya,Semih Çağlar,Yusuf Yiğit Korkmaz,Eray Tüzün*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ATIBA是一个论文稿件检查工具，对稿件执行五项基于证据的完整性和质量检查（参考文献完整性、投稿合规性、实证标准合规性、AI审稿、引文建议），核心原则是LLM只做判断、不凭空生成证据。通过13位非作者参与者的用户研究，六项调查指标一致率为69%-92%，均值85%，初步验证了感知有用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稿件检查（引用完整性、投稿规则合规性、社区报告标准遵循）目前是人工的、重复的，且因期刊/会议而异，实践中常被不一致地执行或完全跳过，缺乏自动化工具。

Method: ATIBA工具实施五项检查：(1) 引用完整性——对照书目来源验证每条引文并标记撤稿或无法找到的参考文献；(2) 投稿合规性——直接从venue的可调用论文页面提取提交标准并据此评估，每个结论锚定到该页面的逐字引用；(3) 实证标准合规性——对照ACM SIGSOFT实证标准，配备幻觉防御（丢弃无法在稿中逐字定位的证据引文）；(4) 多模式AI审稿（指定venue、正式、页面锚定注释），由GPT-5.4（通过Azure OpenAI）驱动；(5) 引文建议功能——推荐候选参考文献并在显示给用户前对照书目来源逐一验证。所有检查围绕同一原则：LLM仅被信任做判断，绝不被信任凭空发明其评判所依据的证据。

Result: 通过有13位非作者参与者参与的受控用户研究进行评估。六项调查指标的一致率为69%-92%，均值85%，提供了跨评估工作流中积极感知有用性的初步证据。但客观准确性尚未测量。

Conclusion: ATIBA在感知有用性方面获得了初步积极证据（平均85%的一致性），确立了感知有用性；客观准确性仍待测量。

Abstract: Checking a manuscript's reference integrity, its compliance with a target venue's specific submission rules, and its adherence to community reporting standards is manual, repetitive, and different for every venue so in practice it is done inconsistently or skipped. We present ATIBA, a tool that runs five grounded integrity and quality checks on a manuscript: a reference-integrity check that verifies each citation against bibliographic sources and flags retracted or unfindable references; a venue/track compliance check that derives submission criteria directly from a venue's own call-for-papers page and evaluates the manuscript against them, each verdict anchored to a verbatim quote from that page; an empirical-standards compliance check against the ACM SIGSOFT Empirical Standards, with a hallucination defence that discards any evidence quote it cannot locate verbatim in the manuscript; a multi-mode AI review (venue-specific, formal, and page-anchored annotation) powered by GPT-5.4 through Azure OpenAI; and a citation-suggestion feature that proposes candidate references for a manuscript and verifies each against bibliographic sources before it is shown to the user. All five checks are designed around the same principle: an LLM is only trusted to judge, never to invent the evidence it judges against. We evaluated ATIBA through a moderated user study with 13 non-author participants. Agreement across the six survey items ranged from 69% to 92%, with a mean of 85%, providing initial evidence of positive perceived usefulness across the evaluated workflows. These findings establish perceived usefulness; objective accuracy remains to be measured.

</details>


### [100] [SWE-Gate: Passing Functional Tests Is Not Enough for Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04167)
*Xin He,Yanlin Wang,Mingwei Liu,Jiachi Chen,Hongyu Zhang,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SWE-Gate，一个仓库级别的软件工程智能体基准测试，首次将真实代码评审意见中提炼的评审约束与功能正确性并列评估。构建了覆盖75个Python开源仓库的303个修复实例，实验发现644个通过功能测试的修复中有221个未满足评审约束，表明仅基于功能的评估会高估智能体的完整任务完成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级软件工程基准（如SWE-bench）仅用功能测试判断补丁是否通过，忽略了真实开发中代码评审意见所蕴含的验收约束（如代码风格、性能、可维护性等非功能要求），导致对编码智能体能力的评估不全面、不真实。
